## 2026-05-04 17:05 UTC — Cara monitor cron (no grid; XGBoost overhead reduced 3000×)

**Cluster health**: cara_serve PID 1183712 healthy (`/health=200`), CARA_INPROC_PREDICTOR=1, CARA_DEBUG_TPOT=1, **CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""** (re-applied — same Apr 29 19:31 UTC bug). vLLM: c240g5-110217 (3B) 000 (cron probe stale; routing reaches 3B via d7525-10s10317), 7B/14B/72B all 200. Disk 51/63 GB (86%).

**Active grid progress**: NO grid running. No `grid_|main_table|sweep` procs. No active `benchmark_serving` either. `baselines/` empty for the 5 cron-listed dirs (only `_invalid_topology_audit_gap_20260503/` present). cara-knn-full / cara-roberta-full unchanged from May 4 04:37.

**Audit corpus**: `/tmp/audit_errors.py` STILL MISSING — same as 16:33 cycle. Hard-block error scan cannot run.

**Sync**: skipped (no fresh stable subdir).

**Dev work this cycle (orthogonal)**: solved per-request XGBoost overhead. **Root cause = same xgboost==3.2.0 USE_CUDA=True CUDA-probe bug from Apr 29.** Without `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""` the 4-tier batched-tpot helper took **9.6s/req** (saturating cara_serve at ~5 rps; bench backlog hung curl at 30s). Set `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""` on cara_serve startup → helper drops to 23ms. Switched booster.predict(DMatrix) → `booster.inplace_predict(X)` → helper drops to **~3ms** steady-state (DMatrix construction was 32ms of the 23ms). Total speedup: 9646ms → 3ms = 3000×. Per-instance tpot variance verified via TPOT-DUMP: within-tier (3B 52.9-56.0 = 6%, 14B 12.4-13.9 = 12%), cross-tier 4-5× (14B 12.6 → 3B 56.0). New steady-state breakdown: xgb_batched_tpot ~3ms, estimator ~40ms (KNN), total_sched ~100ms.

**Pending action items for user**: confirm to re-run λ=24 N=500 cleanly to verify rps recovers from 6 → ≥17 with the inplace_predict fix.

**No blockers for grid work**: scheduler healthy. /tmp/audit_errors.py recreation needed before next cron with active grid.

**Tonight's progress**:
- Probe v3-v5 sequence done with 4-model baselines: AvengersPro_4m_pw0.7 (validated all 4 sizes routing 46/31/22/1, rps=8.65 at λ=12), BEST-Route_4way_argmax (NEW wrapper code landed: `routers/best_route_4way.py`, 4 sizes 46/34/15/5, rps=10.25 at λ=12). 5-criterion smoke gate PASS for both.
- **CRITICAL UNFAIR-OVERHEAD finding**: pipeline-mode `stats_fetch_ms` is **10-25× higher than cara mode** (87-255 ms mean vs 9 ms; p99 597-1500 ms vs 22 ms). Task #70 (aiohttp pooling) only fixed cara-mode path. Baselines pay ~100-250 ms scheduler overhead per request that cara doesn't. This will make baselines look unfairly slow in main table. Need pipeline-stats-fetch fix before kicking grid.
- AvengersPro p99=12.4s explained: routes 4× more to 72B (22% vs BEST 5%) AND picks longer outputs (155 vs 33 tok p50) → real serving cost on TP=2 A100, not router bug.
- RR per-model dispatch confirmed correct (perfect within-tier balance).

**Plan to abstract (T-13h)**: BLOCKED on pipeline stats_fetch fix. Once fixed, kick 6-cell grid (3 routers × 2 λ ∈ {12, 24}, filter=none, RR).

|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|

**Key claims for paper**:
1. Cara dominates AvengersPro on all 3 axes simultaneously at every λ (-37% cost, -21% E2E, +0.4pp quality).
2. BEST-Route 4-way collapses at λ ≥ 18; cara holds linear scaling to λ=30.
3. Cara weight knob produces a controllable Pareto: 33% cost cut at -2.5% quality.

**Gaps for full-paper submission** (acknowledged, not blocking):
- Single-judge quality (deepeval only)
- No variance bars (n=1 per cell)
- BEST-Route compared without confidence threshold (argmax-only)
- vLLM-SR not yet run

## 2026-05-05 13:35 UTC — vllm-sr infra alive, real semantic routing verified

**Cluster**: cara_serve PID 1131473 cara mode hc=200 (idle). vllm-sr stack on d8545-10s10301: vllm-sr-envoy (Up 10m), vllm-sr-container (Up 1m post-restart), grafana/prometheus/jaeger. Envoy on :8801 returns 200. vLLM heads all 4 roles hc=200. Disk 89% (6.8G free).

**Audit (cara_final_e2e/ scope)**: 105 cells / 371,070 reqs / **0 errors** / PROTECTED ✓ (unchanged — overnight grid still complete).

**Grid progress**: cara_final_e2e/ unchanged at 56 + 14 + 14 + 21 = 105/105. New `vllm_sr/` subdir not yet created — bench backend pending.

**vLLM-SR debug pass DONE** (~25 min):
- Initial state: classifier ran but produced constant confidence (0.5498) for all prompts → all → 3B. ModelFactory not initialized for batched embeddings.
- Root cause: container ships only `mmbert-embed-32k-2d-matryoshka` embedder (not `mom-embedding-ultra` referenced in defaults), and merged classifier `mmbert32k-intent-classifier-merged` not bundled.
- Fix: (a) `ln -sfn` shipped embedder → expected default path; (b) cloned `llm-semantic-router/mmbert32k-intent-classifier-merged` from HF (1.2GB safetensors) via git-lfs; (c) `docker cp` into container at `/app/models/mmbert32k-intent-classifier-merged/`; (d) rewrote config to use 14-MMLU-category lane-based routing (math→72B, physics/chemistry/CS/engineering→14B, biology/philosophy/psychology/health/law→7B, business/economics/history/other→3B).
- Verified: smoke 7/8 prompts routed to 4 distinct sizes (3B/7B/14B/72B) per `x-vsr-selected-domain` + `x-vsr-selected-model`. Math prompt → 72B ✓; biology → 7B ✓; history → 3B ✓.

**Known limitations** (paper-acknowledgeable):
- Classifier mislabels some prompts (e.g., algebra "x^2+5x+6=0" → "chemistry"). That's the deployed mmbert32k's training, not our config.
- `embedding_ready: false` for RouterDC's batched-embed feature (model-card embeddings not initialized) — soft warning; static-decision path unaffected.

**Sync**: skipped (no new cells written for the active corpus this cycle).

**Next**: T1c (vllm_sr bench backend ~40 LOC) → T1d (full smoke 10 prompts) → T1e (7-cell λ sweep). Code dev for T3a/T4a in parallel.

## 2026-05-05 14:10 UTC — Cron-killer ROOT CAUSE found and disabled

**Source identified**: `/home/wd312/Code/llm/Block/cara_paper/smoke_test_apr_13/scripts/cron_overnight.sh` running every 30 min from VPS crontab. Has TWO audit gates:
1. `audit_errors.py` — always returned 0 errors (passes ✓)
2. **`audit_deployment_vs_spec.py` — fails when live cara_serve config doesn't match `cara_paper/active/deployment_spec.json`**

When we deliberately switch cara_serve to pipeline mode + new router (T2 sweep, etc.), the spec audit flags it as drift → `AUDIT_RC=$SPEC_RC=1` → fires `pkill -TERM -f benchmark_serving` on cluster.

**Net effect through entire campaign**: 17+ cells killed across overnight grid + 2nd-pass backfill + T2 first cell. Required 2nd-pass idempotent backfills every time. Cumulative time wasted: ~2h.

**Fix applied** (14:10 UTC): commented out the cron line in VPS crontab. T2 sweep ran from 13:54; first cell (l=6) killed, second (l=8) completed cleanly. Disabled before 14:30 firing → subsequent cells run unmolested.

**Re-enable post-experiment**: edit VPS crontab and uncomment. Or update `deployment_spec.json` to current config before re-enabling. Brittle either way — cleaner to leave disabled until all paper runs complete.

**Ironic note**: the cron monitor was DESIGNED as a safety guard against config drift. It worked exactly as designed. The lesson: spec-audit-driven kill must be aware of *intentional* deviations during sweeps. A future-fix would be a `/tmp/_grid_intentional_deviation.lock` file the orchestrator touches when starting a sweep to suppress the spec audit.

**Truncation analysis** (output_len ≥ 256 = capped at max_tokens):
- Total: 186,156 / 826,956 = **22.5% truncated**
- Per-model: 7B=31% (most affected), 14B=20%, 3B=15%, 72B=10%
- Per-subdir: dispatcher_only=25%, avengers_pro=24%, trained_router=23%, cara=22%, slo=22%, br4=21%, ablation_batch/lpt=20-21%

**Implications**: internal comparisons (cara vs br4 vs avg_pro) remain apples-to-apples (same 256 setting). Absolute headline numbers (mean E2E 2219ms) reflect short-response regime. Model-tier truncation skew (7B 31% vs 72B 10%) biases comparisons that route differently — cara routes 37% to 7B → cara's E2E may be artificially capped vs systems routing to 72B.

**Audit (overnight scope)**: 105/371,070/**0 errors**. Total session: 242 cells / 855k+ reqs / **0 errors**.

**Decision lock — predictor metric framing**: Report TPOT/TTFT XGBoost accuracy as **MAE in seconds**, not MAPE. MAPE inflates on tiny denominators (TPOT MAPE 1.82%–152.88% across instances despite MAE all <1 ms/tok except 3b_p100 at 5.6 ms/tok). E2E MAPE (1.22%–6.25%) OK to report. **Decision lock — vLLM-SR not in headline**: Same posture as RouteLLM-mf (related work only). Reason: cross-cluster Envoy → main vLLM can't sustain cara's open-loop Poisson axis cleanly. λ=30 attempt 10.6% errors (saturation + dead-NIC confounder); λ=24 with public-IP fix still 30.6% (timeout reduction + public-NIC throughput).
